Δ_product = ∑ [ (old_i+delta_i)(old_j+delta_j) - old_i old_j ] where j=i+off. Expand: ∑ old_i delta_j + old_j delta_i + delta_i delta_j.

 original linear_i = old_i; new linear_i = old_i + dp (0/d/-d).
 original product_i = old_i*old_j; new product_i = (old_i+di)*(old_j+dj).
 C_off term = linear_i - product_i.
 Contribution change = (di - (old_i+di)(old_j+dj)+old_i old_j) = di - old_i dj - old_j di - di dj.
= (1 - old_j) di - old_i dj - di dj.
If both di,dj nonzero maybe up to 2. Since di,dj are multiples of d (ip=+d, iq=-d), and at most one of di,dj can be nonzero unless i and off relation? If i=p and j=q: di=d, dj=-d => change = (1-old_q)d - old_p(-d) - d*(-d) = (1-old_q + old_p)d + d^2. So B +1 as expected.

   j=i+off
   if i==p or j==p or i==q or j==q (by construction)
   di = d if i==p else -d if i==q else 0
   dj = d if j==p else -d if j==q else 0
   old_i=old[i], old_j=old[j]
   change_coeff_A += (1 - old_j)*(coi) - old_i*(coj) where coi is coefficient of d for di (1 if p, -1 if q, 0), coj similarly.
   change_coeff_B += -(coi*coj)  (since -di dj = - coi coj d^2)

Let's write pair_update function:
```
def pair_update(h, C, n, p, q, iters=32, tol=1e-15, try_candidates=True):
    if p==q: return h,C,False
    oldp=h[p]; oldq=h[q]
    base=(oldp+oldq)*0.5
    dcur=oldp-oldq
    lo=-min(base,1-base); hi=min(base,1-base)
    if hi <= lo + 1e-14: return h,C,False
    Mp=max(p, n-1-p); Mq=max(q, n-1-q)
    off_min=-max(Mp,Mq); off_max=max(Mp,Mq)
    # affected offs integers off_min..off_max
    # Build arrays off_indices = np.arange(off_min, off_max+1)
    # Need C0 = C[off_indices+n-1]
    # A,B arrays length m
```
But constructing np.arange per pair many allocations. Could use list. m up to 1400, okay.

def pair_intervals(T, L0,R0, C0,A,B, unaff, tol=1e-14):
    if unaff > T + tol: return None
    L=L0; R=R0
    forbid_L = -1e300; forbid_R = -1e300; has_forbid=False
    for k in range(len(C0)):
       c=C0[k]; a=A[k]; b=B[k]
       if b > tol:
          disc = a*a - 4*b*(c - T)
          if disc < -tol: return None
          if disc < 0: disc=0
          s=np.sqrt(disc)
          r1=(-a-s)/(2*b); r2=(-a+s)/(2*b)
          if r1 > L: L=r1
          if r2 < R: R=r2
          if L > R + tol: return None
       elif b < -tol:
          disc = a*a - 4*b*(c - T)  # b negative, disc formula
          if disc >= -tol:
             if disc <0: disc=0
             s=np.sqrt(disc); denom=2*b (negative)
             r1=(-a+s)/denom? Need roots of b d^2 + a d + (c-T)=0. formula (-a ± sqrt(disc))/(2b). Since b<0, r1? smaller root = (-a + sqrt)/(2b) (because denom negative, plus sqrt gives more negative?) Let's compute generally: root1=(-a - sqrt)/(2b), root2=(-a + sqrt)/(2b). If b<0, one maybe smaller. We can sort: r=( (-a - s)/(2b), (-a + s)/(2b) ); r1=min(r), r2=max.
             if r1 > L0? store forbid r1,r2.
          # if disc<0 all feasible
       else:
          if abs(a) <= tol:
             if c > T + tol: return None
          elif a > 0:
             R = min(R, (T-c)/a)
             if L > R + tol: return None
          else:
             L = max(L, (T-c)/a)
             if L > R + tol: return None
    if has_forbid:
       r1 = min(forbid_L, ...); r2 = max? Since only one licave, just one.
       if L > r1 + tol and R < r2 - tol: return None
    return (L,R,has_forbid,forbid_r1,forbid_r2)
```

Single_update function top-level:
```
def single_update(h, C, n, p, iters=36, cur_max=None):
    old=h[p]
    if old <=1e-14 or old>=1-1e-14: return h,C,cur_max,False
    M=max(p,n-1-p); off_min=-M; off_max=M
    offs=np.arange(off_min,off_max+1)
    idx=offs+n-1
    C0=C[idx].copy()
    A=np.empty(len)
    B_arr=np.zeros(len)
    for k,off in enumerate(offs):
       # unique i candidates p, p-off
       ai=0.0
       # i=p
       if 0 <= p and 0 <= p+off <= n-1:
           j=p+off; oi=old; oj=C? no old[j]
           ai += 1 - oj
       # i=p-off
       i=p-off
       if i != p and 0 <= i and 0 <= i+off <= n-1: # i+off=p
           oi=old[i]; oj=old
           ai += -oi
       # if off=0, our two i candidates p and p (skip second) because i=p, but B? Need handle B separately. For off=0, linear derivative 1-2old. Our first condition i=p: j=p, oi=old, oj=old -> ai +=1-old. There is no second (i=p skip). Need subtract old*? The formula A=(1-old_j)*coi - old_i*coj with coi=1,coj=1 -> 1-old - old =1-2old. But since i=p appears both as first and second in same term, our unique-i method misses second because same i. For single coordinate, if off=0 and i=p, j=p, both di and dj equal δ, but in same i term. Our loop by unique i cannot represent both. Need special-case off=0: A = 1 - 2*old.
```
For off≠0:
- if p as first: i=p, j=p+off valid: A += 1 - h[j]
- if p as second: i=p-off, j=p valid: A += -h[i]
If both valid (off between -? p as first and second possible if p with off both: p+off valid and p-off valid, e.g., off small and p not edge): sum.
So precompute A easily. B= -1 if off==0 else 0.
```
if off==0: A[k]=1-2*old
else:
  a=0
  j=p+off
  if 0<=j<n: a += 1 - h[j]
  i=p-off
  if 0<=i<n: a += -h[i]
  A[k]=a
```

Detailed single_update:
```
def single_update(h, C, n, p, cur_max, iters=38):
    old = float(h[p])
    if old < 1e-14 or old > 1.0-1e-14: return h,C,cur_max,False
    M = max(p, n-1-p)
    offs = np.arange(-M, M+1, dtype=int)  # creates array each call
    idx = offs + n - 1
    C0 = C[idx].copy()  # counts
    # A for each off
    A = np.empty_like(C0)
    # Determine off=0 index = M (since offs from -M to M)
    # For off != 0:
    # p+off valid:
    j = p + offs
    valid_first = (j >= 0) & (j < n)
    # p-off valid:
    i = p - offs
    valid_second = (i >= 0) & (i < n)
    vals_first = 1.0 - h[j[valid_first]]
    vals_second = -h[i[valid_second]]
    A[valid_first] += vals_first
    A[valid_second] += vals_second
    off0_idx = M
    A[off0_idx] = 1.0 - 2.0*old  # override
    # For off=0, valid_first and second both true and we added 1-old - old =1-2old, same; override okay.
    # unaff max:
    idx_min = n-1 - M; idx_max = n-1 + M
    unaff=0.0
    if idx_min >0: unaff=max(unaff, float(np.max(C[:idx_min])))
    if idx_max+1 < C.size: unaff=max(unaff, float(np.max(C[idx_max+1:])))
    # linear masks excluding off0 and |A|>tol
    lin = np.ones(len(offs), dtype=bool); lin[off0_idx]=False
    pos = lin & (A > 1e-13)
    neg = lin & (A < -1e-13)
    zero = lin & ~pos & ~neg
    zero_minC = float(np.min(C0[zero])) if zero.any() else -1e300
```

Function call overhead maybe okay. We can define top-level `single_check(T, C0,A,pos,neg,zero_min, off0_idx, old, unaff, tol)`. It uses vectorized min. def single_intervals(T, C0, A, pos, neg, zero_min, off0_c, off0_a, old, unaff, tol=1e-13):
   if unaff > T + tol or zero_min > T + tol: return None
   # linear
   L=0.0; R=1.0
   if np.any(pos):
      val = np.min((T - C0[pos]) / A[pos])
      if val < R: R=val
   if np.any(neg):
      val = np.max((T - C0[neg]) / A[neg])
      if val > L: L=val
   if L > R + tol: return None
   # off0
   # Need constraint C0off + A0*(x-old) - (x-old)^2 <= T
   # We can transform to x - x^2 <= K, K=T - (off0_c - old*(1-old))
   K = T - (off0_c - old*(1.0-old))
   # Actually off0_c - old*(1-old) = other. Then x(1-x) <= K.
   disc = 1.0 - 4.0*K
   hasf=False; r1=r2=0
   if disc >= -tol:
      if disc < 0: disc=0
      s=np.sqrt(disc)
      r1=(1-s)/2; r2=(1+s)/2
      hasf=True
      # allowed x <= r1 or x >= r2
      if L > r1 + tol and R < r2 - tol: return None
   return (L,R,hasf,r1,r2)
```

Let's implement single CD:
```
def single_cd(h, n, max_passes, deadline, rng, random=True, full=True, report=False):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    C=np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full')
    cur_max=float(np.max(C))
    dx=2/n
    for pass in range(max_passes):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       order = rng.permutation(n) if random else range(n)
       improved=0
       for p in order:
          if time.time()>deadline: break
          h,C,cur_max,upd = single_update(...)
          if upd: improved+=1
       if improved==0: break
       # project and recompute
       h_proj=project_feasible(h,n)
       # if projection changed significantly, recompute C
       if np.max(np.abs(h_proj-h))>1e-14 or True:
          C=np.correlate(h_proj,1-h_proj,mode='full')
          cur_max=float(np.max(C))
    return h, cur_max*dx
```
But recomputing C at end every pass with np.correlate O(n^2) (fast). We can also if sum drift small skip? Always safe.

```
def project_feasible(h,n):
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   target=n/2.0
   # if within 1e-12, adjust by small distribution
   for iterations:
      diff=target-h.sum()
      if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
      # robust: if diff>0 add to coordinates with room <=1; if all full? diff>0 impossible unless sum<target and some room; if not, break
      # if diff<0 subtract from coordinates >0
   # final exact: while sum diff >1e-12, distribute to one coordinate? Use fractional adjustment to a coordinate not at bound.
```
Previous okay. Add final exact by adjusting a coordinate with slack and not causing large objective? For validity, sum must be exactly n/2? ```
def exact_sum(h,n):
  target=n/2
  for _ in range(100):
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
    if diff>0:
       mask=h<1-1e-12
    else: mask=h>1e-12
    if not mask.any(): break
    # distribute proportional to slack/all values but clip may not exact. Use simple: add diff/count? may clip.
    if diff>0:
       slack=(1-h)[mask]
      tot=slack.sum()
       if tot <=0: break
       h[mask]=np.clip(h[mask]+diff*slack/tot,0,1)
    else:
       val=h[mask]; tot=val.sum(); if tot<=0 break; h[mask]=np.clip(h[mask]+diff*val/tot,0,1)
  # final exact adjust one coordinate
  diff=target-h.sum()
  if abs(diff)>1e-15:
     if diff>0:
       idx=int(np.argmax(1-h))
       h[idx]=min(1.0, h[idx]+diff)
     else:
       idx=int(np.argmax(h))
       h[idx]=max(0.0, h[idx]+diff)
  return h
```

Implementation details for single_cd with deadline:
```
def run_single_cd(h, n, deadline, rng, max_passes=None, min_time=...):
    t_start? Use global time.
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    C=corr(h,n)
    cur_max=float(np.max(C))
    passes=0
    last_improve=time.time()
    while time.time() < deadline - 5:
       pass_start = time.time()
       order = rng.permutation(n) if use_random else np.arange(n)
       improved=0
       for p in order:
          if time.time() > deadline: break
          h,C,cur_max,upd = single_update(h,C,n,p,cur_max)
          if upd: improved+=1
       passes+=1
       # project and recompute
       hp=project_feasible(h,n)
       if np.linalg? maybe always recompute
       C=np.correlate(hp,1-hp,mode='full')
       cur_max=float(np.max(C))
       h=hp
       if improved==0: break
       # If no max improvement after projection? track.
   return h, cur_max*2/n
```

def compute_c5(h,n): return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full'))*(2.0/n))
```

`random_fourier_noise(n, M, rng, amp)`:
```
def random_fourier_noise(n, M, rng, amp):
    idx = 1 + np.arange(n)
    y = np.zeros(n)
    for m in range(1, M+1):
       a = rng.normal()
       b = rng.normal()
       th = 2*np.pi*m*idx/float(n)
       y += a*np.cos(th) + b*np.sin(th)
    mx = abs(y).max()
    if mx > 1e-12: y = y / mx
    return amp * y
```
Check sum zero? Sum of sin/cos over i=1..n: yes.
Could add phase by coefficients random includes.

`smooth_noise` maybe use FFT: random lowfreq via inverse FFT? But above okay.

`interpolate_to_n(h_old,n_old,n_new)`:
```
def interpolate_to_n(h_old, n_old, n_new):
    if n_old == n_new: return h_old.copy()
    x_old = (np.arange(n_old)+0.5)*2.0/n_old
    x_new = (np.arange(n_new)+0.5)*2.0/n_new
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, h_old)
```
Then project.

```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); t_deadline=t0+budget_s-5
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    # get best initial
    try: h_init=np.array(initial_h_values,float).copy()
    except: h_init=default_h(700,rng)
    n_init=len(h_init)
    best_h,best_c,best_n = refine_single(h_init,n_init,0.25*budget_s? maybe 300s, rng)
    # Try perturbations
    for epoch in range(...): if time > deadline-150 break
       # if best_c <= target? continue polish
       # generate perturbation of best_h: amp = 0.02 + 0.08*(epoch/max)
       hp = best_h + amp*random_fourier_noise(best_n,4,rng,1.0)
       # occasionally random low-dim Fourier full: maybe hp = 0.5 + 0.5*tanh(3 * fourier + 0.5*(best_h-0.5)?)
       hp=project_feasible(hp,best_n)
       h1,c1=refine_single(hp,best_n, remaining_budget_chunk, rng)
       if c1<best_c: update
    # try other n values if time
    for n_try in [350,500,840,900?]:
       if time > deadline-120 break
       hn = interpolate_to_n(best_h,best_n,n_try)
       h1,c1=refine_single(hn,n_try, budget_chunk_adaptive, rng)
       if c1<best_c: update
    # final heavy polish on best_n
    if time < deadline-20:
       h1,c1=refine_single(best_h,best_n, until deadline-5, rng, max_passes large)
       if c1<best_c: best=h1,c1,best_n
    # final project and evaluate
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,best_n)
    best_c=compute_c5(best_h,best_n)
    return best_h, float(best_c), best_n
```

# q similarly
j=q+offs; ... Aq...
```

  T=(lo+hi)/2
  feasible = not (unaff > T+tol or zero_min > T+tol)
  if feasible:
     L=dlo; R=dhi
     if pos_any: R=min(R, np.min((T-c_lin[pos])/a_lin[pos]))

  if feasible:
     for kk in conv_idx:
        bbb=b[kk]; aa=a[kk]; cc=c0[kk]
        disc=aa*aa - 4*bbb*(cc - T)
        if disc < -tol: feasible=False; break
        if disc<0: disc=0
        s=sqrt(disc); denom=2*bbb
        r1=(-aa-s)/denom; r2=(-aa+s)/denom
        if r1 > L: L=r1
        if r2 < R: R=r2
        if L>R+tol: feasible=False; break
  if feasible and has_conc:
        bbb=b[conc]; aa=a[conc]; cc=c0[conc]
        disc=aa*aa -4*bbb*(cc-T)
        if disc >= -tol:
           if disc<0: disc=0
           s=sqrt(disc); denom=2*bbb
           r=(-aa-s)/denom; r2=(-aa+s)/denom; r1=min(r,r2); r2=max(r,r2)
           if L > r1 + tol and R < r2 - tol: feasible=False
  if feasible: hi=T else lo=T
```

Function signature:
```
def pair_intersections(T, dlo, dhi, c0, a, b, lin_a, lin_c, pos, neg, zero_min, conv_idx, conc_idx, unaff, tol=1e-12):
    if unaff > T + tol: return None
    if zero_min > T + tol: return None
    L=dlo; R=dhi
    if pos.any(): R=min(R,float(np.min((T - lin_c[pos])/lin_a[pos])))
    if neg.any(): L=max(L,float(np.max((T - lin_c[neg])/lin_a[neg])))
    if L > R + tol: return None
    for k in conv_idx:
       bb=b[k]; aa=a[k]; cc=c0[k]
       disc=aa*aa - 4*bb*(cc - T)
       if disc < -tol: return None
       if disc < 0: disc=0.0
       s=np.sqrt(disc); denom=2*bb
       r1=(-aa - s)/denom; r2=(-aa + s)/denom
       if r1>L: L=r1
       if r2<R: R=r2
       if L>R+tol: return None
    has_conc=False; r1=0.0; r2=0.0
    for k in conc_idx:
       bb=b[k]; aa=a[k]; cc=c0[k]
       disc=aa*aa - 4*bb*(cc - T)
       if disc >= -tol:
          if disc <0: disc=0.0
          s=np.sqrt(disc); denom=2*bb
          ra=(-aa - s)/denom; rb=(-aa + s)/denom
          if ra <= rb: r1, r2 = ra, rb
          else: r1, r2 = rb, ra
          has_conc=True
          if L > r1 + tol and R < r2 - tol: return None
    return (L,R, has_conc, r1, r2)
```
`pos` and `neg` arrays boolean; using .any() each call. Could pass pos_any/neg_any and lin_a[pos] pre-sliced? For vector expressions, need mask. We can precompute `pos_idx = np.nonzero(pos)[0]`, `neg_idx`, and `lin_c_pos`, `lin_a_pos`. Then function uses those arrays to avoid masking each call. Good.
```
def pair_intersections(T,dlo,dhi,c0,a,b, a_pos,c_pos,a_neg,c_neg,zero_min, conv_idx, conc_idx, unaff,tol)
```

Pair_CD implementation:
```
def pair_cd(h,n,max_time_deadline,rng,max_passes=None):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    C=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
    cur_max=float(np.max(C))
    dx=2.0/n
    for pass in range(...):
       if time.time() >= deadline: break
       order = rng.permutation(n)
       pairs = [(order[2*i], order[2*i+1]) for i in range(n//2)]
       improved=0
       for p,q in pairs:
          if time.time()>deadline: break
          h,C,cur_max,upd = pair_update(h,C,n,p,q,cur_max)
          if upd: improved+=1
       if improved==0: break
       # sum check and recompute C? Pair updates preserve sum and C exact, but floating drift. We can skip recompute if improved small? Recompute for safety each pass.
       # However C may drift from exact due rounding; recompute after pass.
       h=project_feasible(h,n) # enforce exact sum/bounds
       C=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); cur_max=float(np.max(C))
    return h, cur_max*dx
```

Code outline:
```
import numpy as np
import time

def compute_c5(h,n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full'))*(2.0/n))

def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    target=0.5*n
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    for _ in range(100):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
       if diff>0:
           m=h<1.0-1e-12
           if not m.any(): break
           slack=(1.0-h)[m]
           tot=slack.sum()
           if tot<=0: break
           h[m]=np.clip(h[m]+diff*slack/tot,0.0,1.0)
       else:
           m=h>1e-12
           if not m.any(): break
           val=h[m]
           tot=val.sum()
           if tot<=0: break
           h[m]=np.clip(h[m]+diff*val/tot,0.0,1.0)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-14:
       if diff>0:
          idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-h))
          h[idx]=min(1.0,h[idx]+diff)
       else:
          idx=int(np.argmax(h))
          h[idx]=max(0.0,h[idx]+diff)
    return h
```
If h length not n? okay.

`pair_intersections` top-level:
```
def pair_intersections(T,dlo,dhi,c0,a,b,a_pos,c_pos,a_neg,c_neg,zero_min,conv_idx,conc_idx,unaff,tol=1e-11):
    if unaff > T + tol or zero_min > T + tol: return None
    L=dlo; R=dhi
    if a_pos.size:
       v=np.min((T-c_pos)/a_pos)
       if v<R: R=float(v)
    if a_neg.size:
       v=np.max((T-c_neg)/a_neg)
       if v>L: L=float(v)
    if L > R + tol: return None
    for k in conv_idx:
       bb=b[k]; aa=a[k]; cc=c0[k]
       disc=aa*aa - 4.0*bb*(cc-T)
       if disc < -tol: return None
       if disc < 0.0: disc=0.0
       s=np.sqrt(disc); denom=2.0*bb
       r1=(-aa-s)/denom; r2=(-aa+s)/denom
       if r1>L: L=r1
       if r2<R: R=r2
       if L>R+tol: return None
    hasf=False; r1=0.0; r2=0.0
    for k in conc_idx:
       bb=b[k]; aa=a[k]; cc=c0[k]
       disc=aa*aa - 4.0*bb*(cc-T)
       if disc >= -tol:
          if disc <0: disc=0.0
          s=np.sqrt(disc); denom=2.0*bb
          r1=(-aa-s)/denom; r2=(-aa+s)/denom
          if r1>r2: r1,r2=r2,r1
          hasf=True
          if L > r1 + tol and R < r2 - tol: return None
    return (L,R,hasf,float(r1),float(r2))
```
For b<0 conc, denom negative; r1/r2 sorted. Good.
For conv b>0, denom positive, r1≤r2? If aa maybe, formula with -aa-s over positive <= -aa+s; yes.

`pair_eval`:
```
def pair_eval(d,c0,a,b,unaff):
    val=unaff
    if c0.size:
       v=c0 + a*d + b*d*d
       m=float(np.max(v))
       if m>val: val=m
    return val
```
`pair_update`:
```
def pair_update(h,C,n,p,q,cur_max,tol=1e-11,iters=38):
    oldp=float(h[p]); oldq=float(h[q])
    if oldp <= 1e-14 or oldp >=1-1e-14 or oldq <=... maybe not skip both? If one at bound, d range maybe nonzero if other interior. For pair constraints 0≤x,y; if base=oldp/2? If one 0, base=oldq/2; domain d∈[-base,base]. So can update. Only skip if base==0 or1 (both same bound) or dhi<=dlo.
    base=0.5*(oldp+oldq); dc=oldp-oldq
    m0=min(base,1.0-base); dlo=-m0; dhi=m0
    if dhi <= dlo + 1e-14: return h,C,cur_max,False
    Mp=max(p,n-1-p); Mq=max(q,n-1-q); M=max(Mp,Mq)
    offs=np.arange(-M,M+1,dtype=np.int64)
    idx=offs+n-1
    c0=C[idx].astype(float, copy=True)
    Ap=np.zeros(offs.size)
    Aq=np.zeros(offs.size)
    j=p+offs; mask=(j>=0)&(j<n); Ap[mask]+=1.0-h[j[mask]]
    i=p-offs; mask=(i>=0)&(i<n); Ap[mask]-=h[i[mask]]
    j=q+offs; mask=(j>=0)&(j<n); Aq[mask]+=1.0-h[j[mask]]
    i=q-offs; mask=(i>=0)&(i<n); Aq[mask]-=h[i[mask]]
    b=np.zeros(offs.size)
    b[offs==0] = -2.0  # offs int array; if off0 in range yes
    dq=q-p
    if dq !=0:
       if -M <= dq <= M: b[dq+M] += 1.0
    mdq=-dq
    if mdq !=0 and -M <= mdq <= M: b[mdq+M] += 1.0
    deltaA=Ap-Aq
    a = deltaA - 2.0*b*dc
    c0 = c0 - deltaA*dc + b*dc*dc
    # bound unaff
    idx_min=n-1-M; idx_max=n-1+M
    unaff=0.0
    if idx_min >0: unaff=max(unaff,float(np.max(C[:idx_min])))
    if idx_max+1 < C.size: unaff=max(unaff,float(np.max(C[idx_max+1:])))
    lin = (b==0.0)
    al=a[lin]; cl=c0[lin]
    pos = al > 1e-12; neg = al < -1e-12
    zero = ~pos & ~neg
    zero_min = float(np.min(cl[zero])) if zero.any() else -1e300
    a_pos=al[pos]; c_pos=cl[pos]; a_neg=al[neg]; c_neg=cl[neg]
    conv_idx=np.nonzero(b>0.0)[0]
    conc_idx=np.nonzero(b<0.0)[0]
    hi = max(float(cur_max), float(unaff)) + 1e-9
    if pair_intersections(hi,dlo,dhi,c0,a,b,a_pos,c_pos,a_neg,c_neg,zero_min,conv_idx,conc_idx,unaff,tol) is None:
        hi = float(np.max(C)) + 1e-7
        if pair_intersections(hi,...) is None:
            return h,C,cur_max,False
    lo = float(unaff)
    # optional lower bound by min possible? not needed
    for _ in range(iters):
       T=0.5*(lo+hi)
       if pair_intersections(T,...) is not None: hi=T
       else: lo=T
    T=hi
    iv=pair_intersections(T+1e-9, ...)
    if iv is None: iv=pair_intersections(hi+1e-7,...)
    if iv is None: return ...
    L,R,hasf,r1,r2=iv
    best_v=cur_max; best_d=dc
    # candidate list: to avoid huge list, use tuple
    cand_list=[]
    for val in (dc,dlo,dhi,0.0,L,R,0.5*(L+R)): cand_list.append(val)
    if hasf:
       for val in (r1,r2,max(dlo,min(r1, dhi?))? actually r1/r2 may outside domain; clip in loop)
          cand_list.append(val)
       # add midpoints of feasible subintervals
       if L <= r1:
          m=0.5*(L+min(r1,dhi)); cand_list.append(m)
       if R >= r2:
          m=0.5*(max(r2,dlo)+R); cand_list.append(m)
    for x in cand_list:
       if x < dlo - 1e-10 or x > dhi + 1e-10: continue
       x=float(np.clip(x, dlo, dhi))
       if abs(x-dc) < 1e-14: continue
       v=pair_eval(x,c0,a,b,unaff)
       if v < best_v - 1e-12:
          best_v=v; best_d=x
    if abs(best_d-dc)<1e-14: return h,C,cur_max,False
    hp=base+best_d; hq=base-best_d
    if hp<0: hp=0; elif hp>1: hp=1 ...
    h[p]=hp; h[q]=hq
    # update C
    C[idx] = c0 + a*best_d + b*best_d*best_d
    if best_v < cur_max: cur_max=best_v
    # maybe if C max > cur_max due numeric, set cur_max=max(cur_max,float(np.max(C)))? That could prevent false improvements. But next coordinate hi uses max(C). We can recompute global max occasionally. Here set cur_max = max(best_v, float(np.max(C)))? If np.max(C)>best_v due numeric or rounding, cur_max should be actual. But if cur_max becomes > previous, update would undo monotonic? We can set cur_max = max(best_v, float(np.max(C))) for safety. If > old cur_max due tiny, maybe okay? But then pair CD might not improve. Use `cur_max = max(best_v, float(np.max(C)))`? O(n) per update expensive. Better set cur_max=best_v and rely. Rounding C values maybe max slightly > best_v by 1e-13. next hi handles if interval fail. okay.
    return h,C,cur_max,True
```

```
def refine_pair_cd(h,n,deadline,rng,iters=38):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    C=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    cur_max=float(np.max(C))
    best_h=h.copy(); best_max=cur_max
    dx=2.0/n
    # quick if n? pass loop
    while time.time() + 0.2 < deadline:
        tpass_start=time.time()
        if tpass_start >= deadline: break
        order=np.random.permutation(n) if rng else...
        improved=0
        if n%2==1: order=order[:n-1]
        pairs=order.reshape(n//2,2) if n even else...
        for i in range(pairs.shape[0]):
            if time.time() >= deadline: break
            p=int(pairs[i,0]); q=int(pairs[i,1])
            h,C,cur_max,upd=pair_update(...)
            if upd: improved+=1
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            # still evaluate current maybe before break?
        h=project_feasible(h,n)
        C=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
        cur_max=float(np.max(C))
        if cur_max < best_max - 1e-15:
            best_max=cur_max; best_h=h.copy()
        if improved ==0:
            break
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,n)
    return best_h, best_max*dx
```

Random perturb/fourier functions:
```
def fourier_noise(n,M,rng,amp):
    y=np.zeros(n)
    idx=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
    for m in range(1,M+1):
        if m > n//2: break
        th=2*np.pi*m*idx/float(n)
        y += rng.normal()*np.cos(th)+rng.normal()*np.sin(th)
    mx=float(np.max(np.abs(y)))
    if mx>1e-12: y*=amp/mx
    return y
```
If sum zero? sum cos/sin zero. amp.

def refine_pair_cd(h,n,deadline,rng):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    C=...; cur_max=...
    best_h=h.copy(); best_max=cur_max
    stag=0
    while time.time() < deadline:
       order=...
       improved=0
       for i...:
          if time.time() >= deadline: break
          ...
       # evaluate current state (inpass) before projection? pair preserves sum; but projection safe
       h=project_feasible(h,n)
       C=np.correlate(...); cur_max=float(np.max(C))
       if cur_max < best_max:
          best_max=cur_max; best_h=h.copy()
       if improved ==0:
          stag+=1
          if stag>=2: break
       else:
          stag=0
       if time.time() >= deadline: break
    h=project_feasible(best_h,n)
    # Optional if current h (best_h) not current after loop? We use best_h.
    return best_h, best_max*dx
```

def make_candidate(h, n, rng, count):
    r = count % 6
    amp = 0.02 + 0.08*min(count,8)/8.0 + 0.02*rng.random()
    if r == 0:
        z = fourier_noise(n,5,rng,1.0)
        return project_feasible(h + amp*z, n)
    if r == 1:
        z = fourier_noise(n,5,rng,1.0)
        fresh = 0.5 + 0.5*np.tanh(2.5*z)
        w = 0.05 + 0.25*count/8.0
        return project_feasible((1.0-w)*h + w*fresh, n)
    if r == 2:
        amp2 = 0.4 + 0.5*rng.random()
        return project_feasible(0.5 + 0.5*amp2*fourier_noise(n,5,rng,1.0), n)
    if r == 3:
        shift = int(rng.randint(1,n-1))
        return project_feasible(np.roll(h, shift), n)
    if r == 4:
        return project_feasible(h + rng.normal(0, amp*0.5, n), n)
    # r==5: frequency higher
    z = fourier_noise(n,8,rng,1.0)
    return project_feasible(h + amp*z, n)
```

Let's write code:

```
import numpy as np
import time

def compute_c5(h,n):
    c=np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c)*(2.0/float(n)))

def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    target=0.5*float(n)
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    for _ in range(100):
        diff=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
        if diff>0:
            m=h<1.0-1e-12
            if not np.any(m): break
            slack=(1.0-h)[m]
            tot=slack.sum()
            if tot<=0.0: break
            h[m]=np.clip(h[m]+diff*slack/tot,0.0,1.0)
        else:
            m=h>1e-12
            if not np.any(m): break
            val=h[m]
            tot=val.sum()
            if tot<=0.0: break
            h[m]=np.clip(h[m]+diff*val/tot,0.0,1.0)
    for _ in range(10):
        diff=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
        if diff>0:
            idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-h))
            h[idx]=min(1.0,h[idx]+diff)
        else:
            idx=int(np.argmax(h))
            h[idx]=max(0.0,h[idx]+diff)
    return h
```

`fourier_noise`:
```
def fourier_noise(n,M,rng,amp=1.0):
    y=np.zeros(n)
    idx=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
    mmax=min(M,n//2)
    for m in range(1,mmax+1):
        th=2.0*np.pi*m*idx/float(n)
        y += rng.normal() * np.cos(th) + rng.normal() * np.sin(th)
    mx=float(np.max(np.abs(y))) if y.size else 0.0
    if mx > 1e-12:
        y *= (amp/mx)
    return y
```
Sum zero? With n//2 maybe if n small okay. If mmax=0 y zero.

`interpolate_to_n`:
```
def interpolate_to_n(old,nold,nn):
    if nold==nn: return np.array(old,dtype=float)
    xo=(np.arange(nold)+0.5)*2.0/nold
    xn=(np.arange(nn)+0.5)*2.0/nn
    return np.interp(xn,xo,old)
```

`make_candidate`:
```
def make_candidate(h,n,rng,count):
    amp=0.02 + 0.10*(count % 10)*0.1 + 0.02*rng.random()
    typ=count % 6
    if typ == 0:
        return project_feasible(h + amp*fourier_noise(n,5,rng,1.0), n)
    if typ == 1:
        z=fourier_noise(n,5,rng,1.0)
        fresh=0.5 + 0.5*np.tanh(2.5*z)
        w=0.05 + 0.30*min(count,8)/8.0
        return project_feasible((1.0-w)*h + w*fresh, n)
    if typ == 2:
        a=0.4 + 0.5*rng.random()
        return project_feasible(0.5 + 0.5*a*fourier_noise(n,6,rng,1.0), n)
    if typ == 3:
        return project_feasible(np.roll(h, int(rng.randint(1,n-1))), n)
    if typ == 4:
        return project_feasible(h + 0.5*amp*rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n), n)
    return project_feasible(h + amp*fourier_noise(n,8,rng,1.0), n)
```

if typ==3:
   if n>3: shift=... else shift=0
```

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    remaining=budget_s
    if remaining < 20: ...
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    deadline=t0+max(5,budget_s-3)
    # initial
    h_init...
    n_init=len(h_init)
    end1 = t0 + min(320.0, max(20.0, 0.28*float(budget_s)))
    if end1 > deadline - 60: end1 = deadline - 60
    best_h,best_c,best_n=refine_pair_cd(h_init,n_init,end1,rng)
    # explorations
    end2 = t0 + min(end1 + 320.0, t0 + 0.55*float(budget_s))
    end2 = min(end2, deadline - 160)
    cnt=0
    while time.time() < end2 - 15 and cnt < 10:
       rem = end2 - time.time() - 5
       per = min(100.0, rem/2.0)
       if per < 25: break
       hp=make_candidate(best_h,best_n,rng,cnt)
       h2,c2,n2=refine_pair_cd(hp,best_n,time.time()+per,rng)
       if c2 < best_c: best_h,best_c,best_n=h2,c2,n2
       cnt +=1
    # n candidates
    end3 = t0 + min(end2 + 320.0, t0 + 0.80*float(budget_s))
    end3 = min(end3, deadline - 80)
    n_cands=[350,500,700,840]
    # sort by distance to best_n
    n_cands=[x for x in n_cands if x != best_n]
    n_cands.sort(key=lambda x: abs(x-best_n)) # lambda not allowed!
```

def detect_corr_sign():
    N=8
    a=np.zeros(N)
    a[0]=0.2; a[-1]=0.7
    v=1.0-a
    c=np.correlate(a,v,mode='full')

    # sign means off = sign * (m - (N-1))? If sign=+ => off=m-(N-1); first off=-(N-1), value a[-1]*(1-a[0])

def detect_corr_sign():
    N=8

```

def pair_update(h,C,n,p,q,cur_max,sign,iters=38,tol=1e-11):
    ...
    M = max(max(p,n-1-p), max(q,n-1-q))
    idx = np.arange(n-1-M, n-1+M, dtype=np.int64)
    offs = sign * (idx - (n-1))
    c0 = C[idx].astype(float, copy=True)
    Ap = np.zeros_like(c0)
    Aq = np.zeros_like(c0)
    j = p + offs; mask=(j>=0)&(j<n); Ap[mask] += 1.0 - h[j[mask]]
    i = p - offs; mask=(i>=0)&(i<n); Ap[mask] -= h[i[mask]]
    j = q + offs; mask=(j>=0)&(j<n); Aq[mask] += 1.0 - h[j[mask]]
    i = q - offs; mask=(i>=0)&(i<n); Aq[mask] -= h[i[mask]]
    b=np.zeros(c0.shape)
    b[M] = -2.0
    dq=q-p
    if dq !=0:
       pos1=M+sign*dq
       pos2=M-sign*dq
       if 0 <= pos1 < b.size: b[pos1]+=1.0
       if 0 <= pos2 < b.size and pos2 != pos1: b[pos2]+=1.0
    # Note if sign=-, pos1=M-sign*dq? Let's use list [dq, -dq]: pos=M+sign*off. For off=-dq, pos=M+sign*(-dq)=M-sign*dq. Good.
```

def pair_intersections(T,dlo,dhi,c0,a,b,a_pos,c_pos,a_neg,c_neg,zero_min,conv_idx,conc_idx,unaff,tol):
    if unaff > T + tol or zero_min > T + tol:
        return None
    L=dlo; R=dhi
    if a_pos.size:
        v=float(np.min((T-c_pos)/a_pos))
        if v < R: R=v
    if a_neg.size:
        v=float(np.max((T-c_neg)/a_neg))
        if v > L: L=v
    if L > R + tol: return None
    for k in conv_idx:
       bb=b[k]; aa=a[k]; cc=c0[k]
       disc=aa*aa - 4.0*bb*(cc-T)
       if disc < -tol: return None
       if disc < 0.0: disc=0.0
       s=np.sqrt(disc); den=2.0*bb
       r1=(-aa-s)/den; r2=(-aa+s)/den
       if r1 > L: L=r1
       if r2 < R: R=r2
       if L > R + tol: return None
    hasf=False; fr1=0.0; fr2=0.0
    for k in conc_idx:
       bb=b[k]; aa=a[k]; cc=c0[k]
       disc=aa*aa - 4.0*bb*(cc-T)
       if disc >= -tol:
          if disc < 0.0: disc=0.0
          s=np.sqrt(disc); den=2.0*bb
          r1=(-aa-s)/den; r2=(-aa+s)/den
          if r1 > r2: r1,r2=r2,r1
          hasf=True; fr1=float(r1); fr2=float(r2)
          if L > fr1 + tol and R < fr2 - tol: return None
    return (L,R,hasf,fr1,fr2)
```

For p=0,q=1, b: off0 pos1=-2? center index1. Cross dq=1, sign=-: pos=M+sign*dq=1-1=0 (off=1), pos2=M-sign*dq=1+1=2 (off=-1). Good. Ap/Aq: offs [1,0,-1]. Now, one subtle point: In pair_update, cross b=+1 for any off where p and q are connected. But what if the actual C_m for that off includes the term h_p h_q with sign maybe plus or minus depending on which array is first? `np.correlate(h,1-h)` always h from first, 1-h from second. For off connecting p to q, term is h_p(1-h_q) = h_p - h_p h_q, quadratic coefficient -1. The pair change h_p=base+d,h_q=base-d gives -[(base+d)(base-d)] = -base^2 + d^2, so B +1. If the connected term is h_q(1-h_p), coefficient also -h_ph_q -> +1. Thus independent. Good.

Self off0: C_0 = ∑ h_i(1-h_i). Pair self terms: -(x^2+y^2) quadratic coefficient -2. Good.

```python
import numpy as np
import time

def compute_c5(h, n):
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / float(n)))

def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(100):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            m = h < 1.0 - 1e-12
            if not np.any(m):
                break
            slack = (1.0 - h)[m]
            tot = float(slack.sum())
            if tot <= 0.0:
                break
            h[m] = np.clip(h[m] + diff * slack / tot, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            m = h > 1e-12
            if not np.any(m):
                break
            val = h[m]
            tot = float(val.sum())
            if tot <= 0.0:
                break
            h[m] = np.clip(h[m] + diff * val / tot, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(10):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            h[idx] = min(1.0, h[idx] + diff)
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            h[idx] = max(0.0, h[idx] + diff)
    return h

def detect_corr_sign():
    N = 8
    a = np.zeros(N)
    a[0] = 0.2
    a[-1] = 0.7
    v = 1.0 - a
    c = np.correlate(a, v, mode='full')
    plus = a[-1] * (1.0 - a[0])
    minus = a[0] * (1.0 - a[-1])
    score_plus = abs(float(c[0]) - plus) + abs(float(c[-1]) - minus)
    score_minus = abs(float(c[0]) - minus) + abs(float(c[-1]) - plus)
    return 1 if score_plus <= score_minus else -1

def fourier_noise(n, M, rng, amp=1.0):
    y = np.zeros(n)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=float)
    mmax = min(M, n // 2)
    for m in range(1, mmax + 1):
        th = 2.0 * np.pi * m * idx / float(n)
        y += rng.normal() * np.cos(th) + rng.normal() * np.sin(th)
    mx = float(np.max(np.abs(y))) if y.size else 0.0
    if mx > 1e-12:
        y *= amp / mx
    return y

def default_h(n):
    z = fourier_noise(n, 5, np.random.RandomState(99), 0.9)
    return project_feasible(0.5 + 0.5 * z, n)

def interpolate_to_n(old, nold, nn):
    if nold == nn:
        return np.asarray(old, dtype=float)
    xo = (np.arange(nold) + 0.5) * 2.0 / nold
    xn = (np.arange(nn) + 0.5) * 2.0 / nn
    return np.interp(xn, xo, old)

def make_candidate(h, n, rng, count):
    amp = 0.02 + 0.10 * min(count, 10) * 0.1 + 0.02 * rng.random()
    typ = count % 6
    if typ == 0:
        return project_feasible(h + amp * fourier_noise(n, 5, rng, 1.0), n)
    if typ == 1:
        z = fourier_noise(n, 5, rng, 1.0)
        fresh = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.tanh(2.5 * z)
        w = 0.05 + 0.30 * min(count, 8) / 8.0
        return project_feasible((1.0 - w) * h + w * fresh, n)
    if typ == 2:
        a2 = 0.4 + 0.5 * rng.random()
        return project_feasible(0.5 + 0.5 * a2 * fourier_noise(n, 6, rng, 1.0), n)
    if typ == 3:
        shift = int(rng.randint(1, n)) if n > 1 else 0
        return project_feasible(np.roll(h, shift), n)
    if typ == 4:
        return project_feasible(h + 0.5 * amp * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n), n)
    return project_feasible(h + amp * fourier_noise(n, 8, rng, 1.0), n)

def pair_eval(d, c0, a, b, unaff):
    val = float(unaff)
    if c0.size:
        v = c0 + a * d + b * d * d
        m = float(np.max(v))
        if m > val:
            val = m
    return val

def pair_intersections(T, dlo, dhi, c0, a, b, a_pos, c_pos, a_neg, c_neg,
                       zero_min, conv_idx, conc_idx, unaff, tol):
    if unaff > T + tol or zero_min > T + tol:
        return None
    L = dlo
    R = dhi
    if a_pos.size:
        v = float(np.min((T - c_pos) / a_pos))
        if v < R:
            R = v
    if a_neg.size:
        v = float(np.max((T - c_neg) / a_neg))
        if v > L:
            L = v
    if L > R + tol:
        return None
    for k in conv_idx:
        bb = float(b[k])
        aa = float(a[k])
        cc = float(c0[k])
        disc = aa * aa - 4.0 * bb * (cc - T)
        if disc < -tol:
            return None
        if disc < 0.0:
            disc = 0.0
        s = np.sqrt(disc)
        den = 2.0 * bb
        r1 = (-aa - s) / den
        r2 = (-aa + s) / den
        if r1 > L:
            L = r1
        if r2 < R:
            R = r2
        if L > R + tol:
            return None
    hasf = False
    fr1 = 0.0
    fr2 = 0.0
    for k in conc_idx:
        bb = float(b[k])
        aa = float(a[k])
        cc = float(c0[k])
        disc = aa * aa - 4.0 * bb * (cc - T)
        if disc >= -tol:
            if disc < 0.0:
                disc = 0.0
            s = np.sqrt(disc)
            den = 2.0 * bb
            r1 = (-aa - s) / den
            r2 = (-aa + s) / den
            if r1 > r2:
                r1, r2 = r2, r1
            hasf = True
            fr1 = float(r1)
            fr2 = float(r2)
            if L > fr1 + tol and R < fr2 - tol:
                return None
    return (L, R, hasf, fr1, fr2)

def pair_update(h, C, n, p, q, cur_max, sign, iters=38, tol=1e-11):
    oldp = float(h[p])
    oldq = float(h[q])
    base = 0.5 * (oldp + oldq)
    dc = oldp - oldq
    m0 = min(base, 1.0 - base)
    dlo = -m0
    dhi = m0
    if dhi <= dlo + 1e-14:
        return h, C, cur_max, False
    Mp = max(p, n - 1 - p)
    Mq = max(q, n - 1 - q)
    M = max(Mp, Mq)
    idx = np.arange(n - 1 - M, n - 1 + M, dtype=np.int64)
    offs = sign * (idx - (n - 1))
    C0 = C[idx].astype(float, copy=True)
    max_aff = float(np.max(C0)) if C0.size else 0.0
    Ap = np.zeros(C0.shape, dtype=float)
    Aq = np.zeros(C0.shape, dtype=float)
    j = p + offs
    mask = (j >= 0) & (j < n)
    Ap[mask] += 1.0 - h[j[mask]]
    i = p - offs
    mask = (i >= 0) & (i < n)
    Ap[mask] -= h[i[mask]]
    j = q + offs
    mask = (j >= 0) & (j < n)
    Aq[mask] += 1.0 - h[j[mask]]
    i = q - offs
    mask = (i >= 0) & (i < n)
    Aq[mask] -= h[i[mask]]
    b = np.zeros(C0.shape, dtype=float)
    b[M] = -2.0
    dq = q - p
    if dq != 0:
        pos1 = M + sign * dq
        pos2 = M - sign * dq
        if 0 <= pos1 < b.size:
            b[pos1] += 1.0
        if 0 <= pos2 < b.size and pos2 != pos1:
            b[pos2] += 1.0
    deltaA = Ap - Aq
    a = deltaA - 2.0 * b * dc
    c0 = C0 - deltaA * dc + b * dc * dc
    idx_min = n - 1 - M
    idx_max = n - 1 + M
    unaff = 0.0
    if idx_min > 0:
        unaff = max(unaff, float(np.max(C[:idx_min])))
    if idx_max + 1 < C.size:
        unaff = max(unaff, float(np.max(C[idx_max + 1:])))
    lin = (b == 0.0)
    al = a[lin]
    cl = c0[lin]
    pos = al > 1e-12
    neg = al < -1e-12
    zero = ~pos & ~neg
    zero_min = float(np.min(cl[zero])) if zero.any() else -1e300
    a_pos = al[pos]
    c_pos = cl[pos]
    a_neg = al[neg]
    c_neg = cl[neg]
    conv_idx = np.nonzero(b > 0.0)[0]
    conc_idx = np.nonzero(b < 0.0)[0]
    hi = max(float(cur_max), float(unaff), max_aff) + 1e-9
    if pair_intersections(hi, dlo, dhi, c0, a, b, a_pos, c_pos, a_neg, c_neg,
                          zero_min, conv_idx, conc_idx, unaff, tol) is None:
        hi = float(np.max(C)) + 1e-7
        if pair_intersections(hi, dlo, dhi, c0, a, b, a_pos, c_pos, a_neg, c_neg,
                              zero_min, conv_idx, conc_idx, unaff, tol) is None:
            return h, C, cur_max, False
    lo = float(unaff)
    for _ in range(iters):
        T = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        if pair_intersections(T, dlo, dhi, c0, a, b, a_pos, c_pos, a_neg, c_neg,
                              zero_min, conv_idx, conc_idx, unaff, tol) is not None:
            hi = T
        else:
            lo = T
    iv = pair_intersections(hi + 1e-9, dlo, dhi, c0, a, b, a_pos, c_pos, a_neg, c_neg,
                            zero_min, conv_idx, conc_idx, unaff, tol)
    if iv is None:
        iv = pair_intersections(hi + 1e-7, dlo, dhi, c0, a, b, a_pos, c_pos, a_neg, c_neg,
                                zero_min, conv_idx, conc_idx, unaff, tol)
    if iv is None:
        return h, C, cur_max, False
    L, R, hasf, r1, r2 = iv
    best_v = float(cur_max)
    best_d = dc
    cands = [dc, dlo, dhi, 0.0, L, R, 0.5 * (L + R)]
    if hasf:
        cands.append(r1)
        cands.append(r2)
        if r1 > L:
            cands.append(0.5 * (L + r1))
        if r2 < R:
            cands.append(0.5 * (r2 + R))
    for x in cands:
        if x < dlo - 1e-10 or x > dhi + 1e-10:
            continue
        x = float(np.clip(x, dlo, dhi))
        if abs(x - dc) < 1e-14:
            continue
        v = pair_eval(x, c0, a, b, unaff)
        if v < best_v - 1e-12:
            best_v = v
            best_d = x
    if abs(best_d - dc) < 1e-14:
        return h, C, cur_max, False
    hp = base + best_d
    hq = base - best_d
    if hp < 0.0:
        hp = 0.0
    elif hp > 1.0:
        hp = 1.0
    if hq < 0.0:
        hq = 0.0
    elif hq > 1.0:
        hq = 1.0
    h[p] = hp
    h[q] = hq
    C[idx] = c0 + a * best_d + b * best_d * best_d
    if best_v < cur_max:
        cur_max = float(best_v)
    return h, C, cur_max, True

def refine_pair_cd(h, n, deadline, rng, sign):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    C = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    cur_max = float(np.max(C))
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_max = cur_max
    stag = 0
    while time.time() < deadline:
        order = rng.permutation(n)
        m = n if n % 2 == 0 else n - 1
        pairs = order[:m].reshape(m // 2, 2)
        improved = 0
        for i in range(pairs.shape[0]):
            if time.time() >= deadline:
                break
            p = int(pairs[i, 0])
            q = int(pairs[i, 1])
            h, C, cur_max, upd = pair_update(h, C, n, p, q, cur_max, sign)
            if upd:
                improved += 1
        h = project_feasible(h, n)
        C = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
        cur_max = float(np.max(C))
        if cur_max < best_max:
            best_max = cur_max
            best_h = h.copy()
        if improved == 0:
            stag += 1
            if stag >= 2:
                break
        else:
            stag = 0
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    return best_h, float(best_max) * (2.0 / float(n)), int(n)

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget = float(budget_s)
    if budget < 25:
        try:
            h0 = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).ravel()
            if h0.size < 2:
                raise ValueError
        except Exception:
            h0 = default_h(700)
        n0 = int(h0.size)
        h0 = project_feasible(h0, n0)
        return h0, compute_c5(h0, n0), n0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    sign = detect_corr_sign()
    deadline = t0 + max(5.0, budget - 3.0)
    try:
        h0 = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).ravel()
        if h0.size < 2:
            raise ValueError
    except Exception:
        h0 = default_h(700)
    n0 = int(h0.size)
    if n0 > 1000:
        h0 = interpolate_to_n(h0, n0, 700)
        n0 = int(h0.size)
    end1 = t0 + min(320.0, max(20.0, 0.28 * budget))
    end1 = min(end1, deadline - 30.0)
    end1 = max(end1, t0 + 10.0)
    best_h, best_c, best_n = refine_pair_cd(h0, n0, end1, rng, sign)
    end2 = min(end1 + 300.0, t0 + 0.60 * budget, deadline - 100.0)
    end2 = max(end2, end1)
    cnt = 0
    while time.time() < end2 - 15 and cnt < 12:
        rem = end2 - time.time() - 5.0
        per = min(110.0, rem / 2.0)
        if per < 30.0:
            break
        hp = make_candidate(best_h, best_n, rng, cnt)
        h2, c2, n2 = refine_pair_cd(hp, best_n, time.time() + per, rng, sign)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = h2, c2, n2
        cnt += 1
    end3 = min(end2 + 300.0, t0 + 0.85 * budget, deadline - 50.0)
    end3 = max(end3, end2)
    n_trials = []
    for x in (350, 500, 700, 840):
        if x != best_n:
            n_trials.append(x)
    for i in range(len(n_trials)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(n_trials)):
            if abs(n_trials[j] - best_n) < abs(n_trials[i] - best_n):
                n_trials[i], n_trials[j] = n_trials[j], n_trials[i]
    done = 0
    for n_try in n_trials:
        if time.time() > end3 - 20:
            break
        rem = end3 - time.time() - 10.0
        per = min(130.0, rem / max(1.0, float(len(n_trials) - done)))
        if per < 30.0:
            break
        if n_try == best_n:
            done += 1
            continue
        hn = interpolate_to_n(best_h, best_n, n_try)
        h2, c2, n2 = refine_pair_cd(hn, n_try, time.time() + per, rng, sign)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = h2, c2, n2
        done += 1
    if time.time() < deadline - 8.0:
        h2, c2, n2 = refine_pair_cd(best_h, best_n, deadline, rng, sign)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = h2, c2, n2
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
